# Skills · H · part 71

856 entries, hxn-gpu-metal to hygiene. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **hxn-gpu-metal**: Drive the GPU from Haxe through Apple's Metal with the hxn-gpu-metal haxelib (package `hxn.gpu.metal`) on Ash, HashLink, hxcpp and CPPIA, on macOS and iOS. Use when a Haxe program runs compute shaders, renders to a texture or a window, or uses shaders written in HXSL with hxnsl on Metal. ([hx-n/hxn](https://github.com/hx-n/hxn/tree/HEAD/site/.well-known/agent-skills/hxn-gpu-metal) · ★3)
- **hxn-gpu-vulkan**: Drive the GPU from Haxe through Vulkan with the hxn-gpu-vulkan haxelib (package `hxn.gpu.vulkan`) on Ash, HashLink, hxcpp and CPPIA, on Linux, Windows, Android and macOS through MoltenVK. Use when a Haxe program runs compute shaders or renders with Vulkan, records barriers and image layout transitions, or uses HXSL shaders from hxnsl as SPIR-V. ([hx-n/hxn](https://github.com/hx-n/hxn/tree/HEAD/site/.well-known/agent-skills/hxn-gpu-vulkan) · ★3)
- **hxn-gpu-webgl2**: Drive the GPU from Haxe in the browser through WebGL2 with the hxn-gpu-webgl2 haxelib (package `hxn.gpu.webgl2`), for hxcpp programs compiled to wasm with `-D emscripten`, threaded or not, and threaded Ash programs (`-D wasm -D hxn_threads`); HashLink and CPPIA do not run in a browser. Use when a Haxe program in a web page renders with WebGL2, for browsers without WebGPU, or uses HXSL shaders from hxnsl as GLSL ES 3.00. ([hx-n/hxn](https://github.com/hx-n/hxn/tree/HEAD/site/.well-known/agent-skills/hxn-gpu-webgl2) · ★3)
- **hxn-gpu-webgpu**: Drive the GPU from Haxe in the browser through WebGPU with the hxn-gpu-webgpu haxelib (package `hxn.gpu.webgpu`), for hxcpp programs compiled to wasm with `-D emscripten`, threaded or not, and threaded Ash programs (`-D wasm -D hxn_threads`); HashLink and CPPIA do not run in a browser. Use when a Haxe program in a web page runs compute shaders or renders with WebGPU, reads buffers back, or uses HXSL shaders from hxnsl as WGSL. ([hx-n/hxn](https://github.com/hx-n/hxn/tree/HEAD/site/.well-known/agent-skills/hxn-gpu-webgpu) · ★3)

- **hydra-gate-admin-router**: Detect admin settings Vue components registered in the vue-router. Admin settings are rendered by Nextcloud's settings framework via `AdminSettings.php`; adding their Vue components to the in-app router exposes them as publicly-accessible frontend routes, bypassing all server-side access checks. ADR-004 hard rule. Observed 2026-04-30 on doriath where `/settings → AdminRoot` was a route in `src/router/index.js` (commit c7c72e9). ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-admin-router) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-composer-audit**: Run `composer audit` to check composer.lock dependencies for known CVEs. Invoked by the builder before push and the reviewer's mandatory block. - **hydra-gate-forbidden-patterns**: Scan lib/ for forbidden debug helpers (var_dump / die / error_log / print_r / dd / dump) that should not ship. Invoked by the builder before push, by the reviewer as Mandatory Step 2, and by the fixer during a retry. Mirrors the orchestrator's `forbidden-patterns` quality gate. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-forbidden-patterns) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-initial-state**: Detect DOM data-attribute reads in `.vue`/`.js`/`.ts` files (e.g. `document.getElementById('x').dataset.version`) used to pull server-side data into the frontend. The Nextcloud-idiomatic pattern is `IInitialState::provideInitialState()` in PHP + `loadState()` from `@nextcloud/initial-state` in Vue. DOM data-attributes break on CSP-hardened instances and bypass the canonical pattern documented in ADR-004. Observed 2026-04-30 on doriath where `AdminRoot.vue` read `document.getElementById('doriath-settings').dataset.version`. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-initial-state) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-modal-isolation**: Detect `&lt;NcModal&gt;` or `&lt;NcDialog&gt;` markup written inline inside parent components. Every modal/dialog MUST live in its own `.vue` file under `src/modals/` (NcModal-based) or `src/dialogs/` (NcDialog-based) and be imported by the parent. Inline modals couple presentation to the parent's lifecycle, prevent reuse, and bloat the parent component. ADR-004 hard rule. Observed 2026-04-30 on doriath. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-modal-isolation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-nc-input-labels**: Detect `&lt;NcSelect&gt;` usages without an `inputLabel` (or `ariaLabelCombobox`) prop. Manual `&lt;label&gt;` elements paired with NcSelect break the component's internal accessibility wiring (WCAG 2.1 AA Success Criteria 1.3.1 and 4.1.2); the built-in props are required for correct screen-reader association. ADR-004 hard rule. Observed 2026-04-30 on doriath. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-nc-input-labels) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-no-admin-idor**: Detect controller methods with `#[NoAdminRequired]` / `@NoAdminRequired` that lack a per-object or admin authorization guard in the method body. Without a guard, any authenticated user can invoke the endpoint on arbitrary object IDs — classic IDOR (OWASP A01:2021 / ADR-005 Rule 3). Observed 2026-04-21 on decidesk#44 where 4 MinutesController methods shipped without guards. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-no-admin-idor) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-orphan-auth**: Detect authorization/validation service methods that are defined but never called. Observed 2026-04-21 on decidesk#60 — `isTransitionAllowed()`, `requiresChairAuthorization()`, `validateQuorum()` were all implemented but never invoked from the state-change caller, leaving dead auth code while the pipeline reported green. A defined-but-unused auth method is identical to having no check at all (OWASP A01:2021). ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-orphan-auth) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-route-auth**: Verify every controller method registered in appinfo/routes.php declares its auth posture via a Nextcloud attribute or docblock tag. Missing `#[PublicPage]` / `#[NoAdminRequired]` / `#[NoCSRFRequired]` / `#[AuthorizedAdminSetting(...)]` silently makes an endpoint unreachable — NC middleware rejects the request before the controller runs. Observed 2026-04-21 on decidesk#47. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-route-auth) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-semantic-auth**: Detect controller methods where the Nextcloud auth annotation does not match the method's actual authorization requirement. Observed 2026-04-23 on decidesk#44 where the builder satisfied gate-5 (route-auth) by adding `#[NoAdminRequired]` to SettingsController::load() even though the method body calls `requireAdmin()`. Gate-5 accepted any auth attribute; this gate catches the semantic mismatch. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-semantic-auth) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-spdx**: Enforce @license + @copyright PHPDoc tags on every PHP file under lib/. Invoked by the builder before push, by the reviewer as the first Mandatory Step, and by the fixer during a retry cycle. Mirrors the orchestrator's `spdx-headers` quality gate exactly. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-spdx) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-stub-scan**: Detect stub code — "In a complete implementation" comments, empty `run()` bodies in BackgroundJob files, unused injected dependencies, hardcoded fetch stubs in Vue `fetch*()` methods. Invoked by the builder before push, the reviewer's mandatory block, and the fixer during retry. Mirrors the orchestrator's `stub-scan` quality gate. ([ConductionNL/shillinq](https://github.com/ConductionNL/shillinq/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hydra-gate-stub-scan) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hydra-gate-unsafe-auth-resolver**: Detect `catch (\Throwable) { return null; }` patterns in service methods that resolve auth/authorization/permission/role services. The nullable return shape enables a silent-fail-open pattern where the caller treats "service unavailable" as "check skipped" (OWASP A01:2021 / CWE-863). Observed 2026-04-21 on decidesk#45 where `DecisionApprovalService::getAuthorizationService()` returned null on Throwable and the caller guarded the role check with `if ($auth !== null)`, letting any authed user advance decisions when the service was briefly unavailable.
